[COMPARISON_RULE_ASCENDING](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Static variable in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
A constant that specifies that the `join` method selects only the elements of the `joined` `Source` that are in the `comparison` `Source` and that the positions of those elements in the resulting `Source` are in the same order as they are in the `comparison` `Source`.

[COMPARISON_RULE_ASCENDING_NULLS_FIRST](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Static variable in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
A constant that specifies that the `join` method selects only the elements of the `joined` `Source` that are in the `comparison` `Source` and that the positions of those elements are in the same order in the resulting `Source` as they are in the `comparison` `Source` except that any `null` elements appear last.

[COMPARISON_RULE_ASCENDING_NULLS_LAST](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Static variable in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
A constant that specifies that the `join` method selects only the elements of the `joined` `Source` that are in the `comparison` `Source` and that the positions of those elements in the resulting `Source` are in the same order as they are in the `comparison` `Source` with any `null` elements appearing last.

[COMPARISON_RULE_DESCENDING](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Static variable in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
A constant that specifies that the `join` method selects only the elements of the `joined` `Source` that are in the `comparison` `Source` and that the positions of those elements in the resulting `Source` are in the reverse order of what they are in the `comparison` `Source`.

[COMPARISON_RULE_DESCENDING_NULLS_FIRST](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Static variable in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
A constant that specifies that the `join` method selects only the elements of the `joined` `Source` that are in the `comparison` `Source` and that the positions of those elements in the resulting `Source` are in the reverse order of what they are in the `comparison` `Source` with any `null` elements appearing first.

[COMPARISON_RULE_DESCENDING_NULLS_LAST](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Static variable in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
A constant that specifies that the `join` method selects only the elements of the `joined` `Source` that are in the `comparison` `Source` and that the positions of those elements in the resulting `Source` are in the reverse order of what they are in the `comparison` `Source` except that any `null` elements appear last.

[COMPARISON_RULE_REMOVE](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Static variable in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
A constant that specifies that the `join` method selects only the elements of the joined `Source` that are not in the comparison `Source`.

[COMPARISON_RULE_SELECT](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Static variable in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
A constant that specifies that the `join` method selects only the elements of the `joined` `Source` that are in the `comparison` `Source`.

[executeBuild(BuildProcess, BuildProperties, BuildResult)](oracle/olapi/data/source/DataProvider.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[DataProvider](oracle/olapi/data/source/DataProvider.md)
Executes the commands of a `BuildProcess` that has the specified properties; the build process generates an identification number for the build and stores it in the `BuildResult`.

[executeBuild(BuildProcess, int, BuildResult)](oracle/olapi/data/source/DataProvider.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[DataProvider](oracle/olapi/data/source/DataProvider.md)
Executes the commands of a `BuildProcess` using the specified number of parallel processes; the build process generates an identification number for the build and stores it in the `BuildResult`.

[executeBuild(MdmNamedBuildProcess, int, BuildResult)](oracle/olapi/data/source/DataProvider.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[DataProvider](oracle/olapi/data/source/DataProvider.md)
Executes the commands of an `MdmNamedBuildProcess` using the specified number of parallel processes; the build process generates an identification number for the build and stores it in the `BuildResult`.

[exp()](oracle/olapi/syntax/TypedExpression.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.syntax.[TypedExpression](oracle/olapi/syntax/TypedExpression.md)
Calculates the value of 2.71828183 raised to the power of the value of this `TypedExpression`.

[oracle.express](oracle/express/package-summary.md) - package oracle.express
Contains classes that support processing in Oracle OLAP.

[oracle.express.olapi.data.full](oracle/express/olapi/data/full/package-summary.md) - package oracle.express.olapi.data.full
Contains an `Exception` class that might be encountered by a client application.

[oracle.express.spl](oracle/express/spl/package-summary.md) - package oracle.express.spl
Contains classes that support the execution of OLAP DML commands in Oracle OLAP.

[oracle.olapi](oracle/olapi/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi
Contains an interface, a class, and exceptions that are of general use in the Oracle OLAP Java API.

[oracle.olapi.data.cursor](oracle/olapi/data/cursor/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.data.cursor
Contains interfaces and classes for retrieving and accessing the result set of a query.

[oracle.olapi.data.source](oracle/olapi/data/source/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.data.source
Contains classes that construct a query of the data in an Oracle Database.

[oracle.olapi.metadata](oracle/olapi/metadata/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.metadata
Contains interfaces and classes that represent Oracle OLAP metadata objects and that provide those objects to an application.

[oracle.olapi.metadata.deployment](oracle/olapi/metadata/deployment/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.metadata.deployment
Contains classes that specify the deployment of a logical OLAP model as relational OLAP (ROLAP) objects or as analytic workspace (AW) objects.

[oracle.olapi.metadata.mapping](oracle/olapi/metadata/mapping/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.metadata.mapping
Contains classes that map the `MdmObject` objects of a logical OLAP metadata model to physical storage structures in an Oracle Database instance.

[oracle.olapi.metadata.mdm](oracle/olapi/metadata/mdm/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.metadata.mdm
Contains classes that represent data supporting the multidimensional model (MDM) for metadata.

[oracle.olapi.resource](oracle/olapi/resource/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.resource
Provides support for the internationalization of messages for `Exception` classes in the Oracle OLAP Java API.

[oracle.olapi.session](oracle/olapi/session/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.session
Contains a class that represents a session that is associated with a specific connection to an Oracle Database instance.

[oracle.olapi.syntax](oracle/olapi/syntax/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.syntax
Contains classes that represent data types, functions, commands, and data objects.

[oracle.olapi.transaction](oracle/olapi/transaction/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.transaction
Contains interfaces that define classes that are involved in transactions between Oracle OLAP and an OLAP client application and `Exception` classes that an application might encounter.

[oracle.olapi.transaction.metadataStateManager](oracle/olapi/transaction/metadataStateManager/package-summary.md) - package oracle.olapi.transaction.metadataStateManager
Contains an interface to implement and associate with a `Template` object.

Sorts the elements of this `Source` in the ascending order of their data type.

[sortAscending(Source)](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
Selects the elements of this `Source` that are specified by the elements of `sortValue`, and orders them in the ascending order of the `sortValue` data type.

[sortAscending(Source, boolean)](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
Selects the elements of this `Source` that are specified by the elements of `sortValue`, and orders them in the ascending order of the `sortValue` data type.

[sortAscendingHierarchically(Source, Source, boolean, boolean)](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
Selects the elements of this `Source` that are specified by `joined` and the descendants of those elements in the hierarchy that this `Source` represents and sorts the children of the parents in ascending order.

[sortAscendingHierarchically(Source, Source)](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
Selects the elements of this `Source` that are specified by `joined` and the descendants of those elements in the hierarchy that this `Source` represents and sorts the children of the parents in ascending order with the parents appearing before their children.

[sortAscendingHierarchically(Source, boolean)](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
Selects the elements of this `Source` and the descendants of those elements in the hierarchy that this `Source` represents and sorts the children of the parents in ascending order.

[sortAscendingHierarchically(Source)](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
Selects the elements of this `Source` and the descendants of those elements in the hierarchy that this `Source` represents and sorts the children of the parents in ascending order with the parents appearing before the children in the result.

[sortDescending()](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md) - Method in class oracle.olapi.data.source.[Source](oracle/olapi/data/source/Source.md)
Sorts the elements of this `Source` in the descending order of their values.

[XML11_2_ParserCallback](oracle/olapi/metadata/XML11_2_ParserCallback.md) - Interface in [oracle.olapi.metadata](oracle/olapi/metadata/package-summary.md)
An extension of the `XMLParserCallback` interface that adds methods for renaming an object and for suppressing attributes of objects.

[XML_FORMAT_CATEGORY](oracle/olapi/syntax/DataType.md) - Static variable in class oracle.olapi.syntax.[DataType](oracle/olapi/syntax/DataType.md)
A constant that represents the XML format category of data types.

A constant that represents the `XMLFormat` data type.

[XMLParserCallback](oracle/olapi/metadata/XMLParserCallback.md) - Interface in [oracle.olapi.metadata](oracle/olapi/metadata/package-summary.md)
An interface for an object that processes errors that occur as an `MdmMetadataProvider.importXML` method produces a metadata object from an XML object definition or as a `SyntaxObject.fromSyntax` method produces a `SyntaxObject`.

[XMLTYPE](oracle/olapi/syntax/DataType.md) - Static variable in class oracle.olapi.syntax.[DataType](oracle/olapi/syntax/DataType.md)

[XMLWriterCallback](oracle/olapi/metadata/XMLWriterCallback.md) - Interface in [oracle.olapi.metadata](oracle/olapi/metadata/package-summary.md)
